<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-49870367" data-id="49870367" data-tags="AI" data-score="1939" data-comments="1079" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49870367" data-time="1790539974">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sancho.bearblog.dev/google-weird/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=sancho.bearblog.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>sancho.bearblog.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sancho.bearblog.dev/google-weird/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When did Google get so weird?</span><span class='zh'>谷歌什么时候变得这么怪了？</span></a><span class="dom">sancho.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has shifted toward bolder experiments and AI-driven features across its products.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在产品上变得更加大胆，推出基于 AI 的新功能与实验性改动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has shifted toward bolder experiments and AI-driven features across its products. The change reflects a broader industry push toward rapid iteration, sometimes at the expense of polish and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在产品上变得更加大胆，推出基于 AI 的新功能与实验性改动。这一趋势与行业对快速迭代的普遍追求相呼应，偶尔会牺牲产品的稳定性与用户期望。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1939</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49870367' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1079</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49913571" data-id="49913571" data-tags="AI" data-score="1681" data-comments="1162" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913571" data-time="1790798677">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-4-argon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 4 Argon（阿尔贡）</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon appears to be a new variant in Google&#x27;s Gemini 4 family, signaling ongoing expansion of the model lineup to cover different price-performance tiers.</span><span class='zh'>这是谷歌在 Gemini 4 系列中的一个新变体，凸显通过分层定价来覆盖不同用例的策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 4 Argon appears to be a new variant in Google&#x27;s Gemini 4 family, signaling ongoing expansion of the model lineup to cover different price-performance tiers. Argon is pitched as a balance between capability and efficiency, giving enterprises a more cost-effective path to scale language-model workloads.</span><span class='zh'>这是谷歌在 Gemini 4 系列中的一个新变体，凸显通过分层定价来覆盖不同用例的策略。Argon 版本强调在性能与效率之间的折中，面向需要大规模语言模型但关注成本的企业场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An account describes being owed a billion dollars in Nvidia stock, spotlighting the liquidity and risk implications of equity-based wealth.</span><span class='zh'>有人被要求以 Nvidia 股票作债务，金额高达十亿美元，揭示以股票为财富的现实风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An account describes being owed a billion dollars in Nvidia stock, spotlighting the liquidity and risk implications of equity-based wealth. When compensation or guarantees hinge on volatile asset prices, personal finances become exposed to market swings and regulatory quirks.</span><span class='zh'>有人被要求以 Nvidia 股票作债务，金额高达十亿美元，揭示以股票为财富的现实风险。股价波动使基于股权的承诺带来流动性与合规方面的挑战，个人资产因此暴露于市场波动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-6-1-sol/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT 6.1 Sol: Near-Astra intelligence for a fifth of the price</span><span class='zh'>GPT-6.1 Sol 发布</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6.1 Sol as an incremental update to the GPT-6 line, focusing on improved reliability, safety controls, and developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-6.1 Sol，作为 GPT-6 系列的增量更新，着力提升稳定性与安全控制，并强化对开发者工具的支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6.1 Sol as an incremental update to the GPT-6 line, focusing on improved reliability, safety controls, and developer tools. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New map visuals show significant destruction in Rafah, highlighting humanitarian needs and reconstruction challenges. The update underscores how real-time mapping supports crisis response and policy awareness.</span><span class='zh'>新的地图图像显示拉法城遭受严重破坏，暴露出人道需求与重建挑战。更新强调实时地图在危机响应与政策关注中的作用。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/the-death-of-the-american-host" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Everybody’s home. No one’s coming over</span><span class='zh'>人人都在家，却没人来访</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Americans are hosting less than before, with more people staying home and fewer dinner invitations circulating.</span><span class='zh'>美国的接待文化正在衰退，越来越多人呆在家里，但请客来访的次数在减少。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Americans are hosting less than before, with more people staying home and fewer dinner invitations circulating. The piece links this to longer work hours, streaming culture, and social fragmentation, and considers what hospitality, community, and local economies lose when in-person gatherings fade.</span><span class='zh'>美国的接待文化正在衰退，越来越多人呆在家里，但请客来访的次数在减少。文章将工作压力、数字娱乐和社交碎片化与线下聚会的下降联系起来，讨论这对社区生活、依赖聚会的小型企业以及何谓“好客”的影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>804</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891295' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>718</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49949438" data-id="49949438" data-tags="Culture" data-score="799" data-comments="173" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438" data-time="1791075052">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Bob Cringely has died</span><span class='zh'>鲍勃·克林西利去世（Bob Cringely）</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</span><span class='zh'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>799</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>173</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49927754" data-id="49927754" data-tags="Legal|Privacy|Politics" data-score="783" data-comments="388" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754" data-time="1790893387">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='eff.org' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Court agrees with EFF: Utah&#x27;s VPN law demands a technical impossibility</span><span class='zh'>法院认定犹他州的 VPN 法等同技术不可实现</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet.</span><span class='zh'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet. The decision underscores how prescriptive tech laws can clash with real-world capabilities of privacy tools. For engineers and policymakers, it&#x27;s a reminder that regulatory designs must align with practical feasibility.</span><span class='zh'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。此判决凸显了规制性科技法规与隐私工具现实能力之间的冲突。对工程师和政策制定者而言，这是一个提醒：可执行的规则须与实际可行性相符。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://america.gov/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>America.gov</span><span class='zh'>America.gov 政府信息门户</span></a><span class="dom">america.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>America.gov consolidates official U.S.</span><span class='zh'>America.gov 将美国政府的官方信息集中到一个门户，提升公众获取政策、数据与服务的便利性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>America.gov consolidates official U.S. government information into a central portal to improve public access to policies, data, and services. The platform aims to streamline citizen engagement and provide clear, official guidance across agencies.</span><span class='zh'>America.gov 将美国政府的官方信息集中到一个门户，提升公众获取政策、数据与服务的便利性。该平台旨在简化公民互动，提供跨机构的清晰官方指引。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-dots/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dots: Always-on agents</span><span class='zh'>Dots：持续运行的智能代理</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils Dots, a framework for persistent, always-on agents that can operate continuously with long term memory and coordinated actions.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 推出 Dots，支持持续运行、具备长期记忆的智能代理，能够持续执行任务并协调行动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils Dots, a framework for persistent, always-on agents that can operate continuously with long term memory and coordinated actions. This could enable more autonomous AI workflows and real-time decision making, but raises questions about safety, resource use, and privacy.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 推出 Dots，支持持续运行、具备长期记忆的智能代理，能够持续执行任务并协调行动。这一能力可能推动更高效的自动化工作流和实时决策，但也引发安全、资源消耗和隐私方面的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kolibri is presented as a sovereign open-weight model, designed for on-premise deployment with privacy and governance controls.</span><span class='zh'>Kolibri 被定位为一款主权开放权重的模型，允许企业在本地部署以实现隐私与治理控制。文章介绍了许可、安全措施以及在私有环境中的部署选项。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StreetComplete on iOS is now in public beta</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete iOS 公测现已开启</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks. The move broadens access beyond Android and could accelerate map data improvements, though testers should expect typical beta churn as the app matures.</span><span class='zh'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。此次扩平台可望推动社区贡献并让 Android 之外的用户加入，但测试阶段仍会有常见的不稳定和本地化需要改进的问题。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/3/default-hard-budget-caps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re going to need default hard budget caps on pretty much everything</span><span class='zh'>我们需要对几乎所有事情设定默认的硬性预算上限</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments. The piece argues for automatic limits on compute, storage, and spending, plus tooling to enforce caps without stifling creativity. It positions budget discipline as essential for sustainable experimentation in AI and cloud-native stacks.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。文章提出在计算、存储等方面设定自动约束，并提供执行工具，既控制支出又不完全扼杀创新，强调预算约束在可持续创新中的作用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</span><span class='zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/delhi-electricity-loss" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67793961&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C250%2C0%2C250' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='spectrum.ieee.org' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/delhi-electricity-loss" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Delhi cut electricity loss from 50 to 5 percent</span><span class='zh'>德里如何将电力损耗从50%降至5%</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering.</span><span class='zh'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering. The move showcases how smart infrastructure and governance can unlock huge efficiency gains for urban utilities and could serve as a scalable model for other cities.</span><span class='zh'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。此举展示了智慧基础设施与治理在提升城市电力效率方面的潜力，并具有可复制性供其他城市参考。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Energy' style='--c:#eab308'>Energy</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>571</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892245' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>309</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49883844" data-id="49883844" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="569" data-comments="222" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883844" data-time="1790627016">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/firelex/jeff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/firelex/jeff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jeff – Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models, trained at home, ~30 ms</span><span class='zh'>Jeff：Jev 兼容的0.8B决策模型，在家训练，推理约30毫秒</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms.</span><span class='zh'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms. The lightweight scale enables on-device experimentation, lowering barriers for individuals and small teams to prototype decision systems.</span><span class='zh'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。此举降低了个人和小团队在本地进行 AI 原型开发的门槛，凸显对隐私和本地化计算友好的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Several vulnerabilities have been discovered in the Linux kernel</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核曝出多项漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">lwn.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage. Patch availability and response timing will vary by distribution, so admins should test updates promptly. The disclosure underscores the ongoing drive to harden core OS components against emerging threat vectors.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。各发行版的修复时间不同，系统管理员应尽快测试并应用更新。此次披露凸显持续对核心操作系统组件进行加固的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/09/draftkings-using-ai-supercharge-harms-online-behavioral-advertising" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to behaviorally target chronic gamblers</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在用 AI 针对慢性赌徒进行行为定向</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending. This raises urgent ethical and regulatory concerns about predatory targeting, calling for stronger safeguards, transparency, and oversight to protect vulnerable users.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。这一做法引发关于掠夺性定向、伦理与监管的紧迫关切，需加强保护措施、透明度和监管以保护脆弱用户。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/coding-is-not-solved" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Coding is not solved</span><span class='zh'>编码问题尚未解决</span></a><span class="dom">blog.alexewerlof.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging. AI can help but cannot replace the craft of software engineering or the need for disciplined practices.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。AI 能帮助，但无法取代软件工程的工艺性与规范性实践。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>From a Polish vantage point, the post surveys how September 2026 reshapes tech policy, AI adoption, and global power dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>从波兰视角出发，文章梳理了2026年9月全球科技政策、AI采用与地缘政治格局的变化。</span></p>

It blends on-the-ground observations with macro signals about EU regulation and startup funding. The takeaway is a cautious view of where innovation and geopolitics intersect.</span><span class='zh'>从波兰视角出发，文章梳理了2026年9月全球科技政策、AI采用与地缘政治格局的变化。结合实地观察与宏观信号，分析欧盟监管与初创资金的趋势。核心在于警觉地看待创新与地缘政治如何交汇。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/sep/29/trial-live-facial-recognition-cameras-london-stations-false-positive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/59a72cd2826be7e1e72f407ba3a515ec43697227/546_44_4210_3370/master/4210.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0c92b09e04de7a9aa71a1822d47b9589' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theguardian.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/sep/29/trial-live-facial-recognition-cameras-london-stations-false-positive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>500k facial scans at UK stations yield no arrests, 1 false positive</span><span class='zh'>英国车站的五十万次人脸识别未逮捕，仅一例误报</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans.</span><span class='zh'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans. The result highlights limited predictive value for crime prevention and rekindles debates over privacy and surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。结果凸显对治安预防的实际效用有限，再次引发对隐私与监控的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Singapore&#x27;s government dating app uses the Gale-Shapley stable marriage algorithm to optimize matches.</span><span class='zh'>新加坡政府的交友应用采用稳定婚配算法（Gale-Shapley）来优化配对。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Singapore&#x27;s government dating app uses the Gale-Shapley stable marriage algorithm to optimize matches. The approach raises questions about privacy, consent, and fairness in algorithmic matchmaking while illustrating how formal matching theory can inform consumer apps.</span><span class='zh'>新加坡政府的交友应用采用稳定婚配算法（Gale-Shapley）来优化配对。该做法引发关于隐私、同意和算法公平性的讨论，同时展示了正式匹配理论在消费级应用中的应用潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thecivilian.co.nz/2026/09/27/ai-companies-in-fierce-arms-race-to-demonstrate-their-model-is-the-most-existentially-threatening-to-humanity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI companies in race to demonstrate their model most threatening to humanity</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司竞相证明其模型对人类威胁最大</span></a><span class="dom">thecivilian.co.nz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI firms are racing to demonstrate which model poses the greatest existential threat to humanity, a framing that influences policy, funding, and public trust.</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正在竞相证明其模型对人类构成最大的生存威胁，这种论调会影响政策、资金与公众信任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI firms are racing to demonstrate which model poses the greatest existential threat to humanity, a framing that influences policy, funding, and public trust. This competition blends safety rhetoric with competitive signaling, sometimes obscuring real risk assessments. The takeaway is that governance and independent evaluation matter as much as capability.</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正在竞相证明其模型对人类构成最大的生存威胁，这种论调会影响政策、资金与公众信任。此类竞赛把安全口号和竞争信号混合在一起，常常掩盖真实风险评估。关键在于治理与独立评估的重要性，与能力两者同等重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jorgegarciaherrero.com/wp-content/interactivos/20260916-Prompt-like-a-butterfly-sting-like-a-tracker-(clean).pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Privacy Analysis of Web and Mobile Conversational AI Agents [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>网络与移动端对话式人工智能代理的隐私分析</span></a><span class="dom">jorgegarciaherrero.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A detailed privacy analysis of web and mobile conversational AI agents reveals how these systems collect data, process transcripts, and may expose users to privacy risks.</span><span class='zh'>对网页及移动端对话式人工智能代理的数据收集、传输与存储等隐私风险进行了深入分析，揭示潜在的敏感信息泄露与第三方数据共享等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A detailed privacy analysis of web and mobile conversational AI agents reveals how these systems collect data, process transcripts, and may expose users to privacy risks. The report highlights issues like data sharing with third parties, retention policies, and potential prompt leakage, underscoring the need for stricter minimization, transparency, and privacy-preserving design.</span><span class='zh'>对网页及移动端对话式人工智能代理的数据收集、传输与存储等隐私风险进行了深入分析，揭示潜在的敏感信息泄露与第三方数据共享等问题。强调需要更严格的数据最小化、透明度与隐私保护设计，以及改进的访问控制与用户权利。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kolibri is Aleph Alpha&#x27;s sovereign LLM designed to run on private infrastructure inside Germany, giving organizations control over data and governance.</span><span class='zh'>Kolibri 是 Aleph Alpha 的自主大语言模型，设计用于在德国的私有基础设施中运行，以实现数据与治理的控制权。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kolibri is Aleph Alpha&#x27;s sovereign LLM designed to run on private infrastructure inside Germany, giving organizations control over data and governance. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of the electrician trade as a model of skilled, hands-on work and problem-solving. The piece contrasts traditional trades with software careers, highlighting discipline, apprenticeship, and safety as transferable skills for technical work. It argues that the craft offers a different path into engineering that rewards practical intuition and precise execution.</span><span class='zh'>本文对电工这一技艺进行了深度探讨，强调动手实践、学徒制和安全意识等技能在技术工作中的价值。与软件行业的常规路径相比，电工的精益求精和实操直觉同样是进入工程领域的有力路径，适合喜欢动手的读者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://insufferable.dev/posts/the-ai-race-just-got-awkward/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The AI Race Just Got Awkward</span><span class='zh'>AI 竞赛突然变得尴尬</span></a><span class="dom">insufferable.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI competition has hit an awkward moment where hype clashes with practical limits, safety, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>AI竞赛进入尴尬阶段，炒作与现实限制造成紧张局面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI competition has hit an awkward moment where hype clashes with practical limits, safety, and governance. Companies push scale while grappling with data, compute costs, and credible benchmarks, underscoring the need for transparent metrics and responsible deployment.</span><span class='zh'>AI竞赛进入尴尬阶段，炒作与现实限制造成紧张局面。各方在追求规模的同时，需面对数据、算力成本与可信基准的挑战，强调需要更透明的评估标准与负责任的落地。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.deepseek.com/en/harness/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Harness Desktop for macOS and Windows</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Harness桌面版（macOS 与 Windows）</span></a><span class="dom">deepseek.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://turbopuffer.com/blog/rip-vector-database" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RIP, vector database</span><span class='zh'>向量数据库：再见</span></a><span class="dom">turbopuffer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative take arguing that the era of dedicated vector databases may be fading as search and embedding work converge.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章质疑专用向量数据库的长期价值，认为搜索与嵌入技术正在合并，成败取决于成本、复杂性和性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative take arguing that the era of dedicated vector databases may be fading as search and embedding work converge. The piece weighs cost, complexity, and performance trade-offs, suggesting that robust vector capabilities can live inside existing databases or newer hybrid engines.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章质疑专用向量数据库的长期价值，认为搜索与嵌入技术正在合并，成败取决于成本、复杂性和性能。作者提出，将向量功能整合到现有数据库或使用混合型引擎，可能比维护独立向量数据库更高效。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ssp.sh/brain/the-problem-is-not-the-ai-code-but-nobody-knows-anything-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The problem is not AI code, but not knowing about system architecture or intent</span><span class='zh'>问题不在AI代码，而在于对体系结构与意图的缺乏了解</span></a><span class="dom">ssp.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The real bottleneck isn’t the AI-generated code but humans’ lack of systemic context.</span><span class='zh'>真正的问题不是 AI 生成的代码，而是对系统架构、目标与上下文的缺乏清晰理解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The real bottleneck isn’t the AI-generated code but humans’ lack of systemic context. Without solid architecture understanding and a clear intent, AI-assisted code can misfit, leading to brittle systems and governance gaps.</span><span class='zh'>真正的问题不是 AI 生成的代码，而是对系统架构、目标与上下文的缺乏清晰理解。缺乏架构层面的把握与明确意图，AI 产物容易不贴合需求，造成系统脆弱与治理漏洞。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/bloomberg-terminal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A brief history of the Bloomberg terminal</span><span class='zh'>彭博终端的简史</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Bloomberg Terminal revolutionized finance by bundling real-time market data, trading tools, and messaging into a single terminal.</span><span class='zh'>彭博终端自1980年代起就把实时市场数据、交易工具和通讯功能打包到一个设备中，彻底改变金融行业的工作流程与信息获取方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Bloomberg Terminal revolutionized finance by bundling real-time market data, trading tools, and messaging into a single terminal. The piece traces its origins in the 1980s, its enduring business model, and how it shaped data licensing and professional workflows.</span><span class='zh'>彭博终端自1980年代起就把实时市场数据、交易工具和通讯功能打包到一个设备中，彻底改变金融行业的工作流程与信息获取方式。文章回顾其发展历程、持久的商业模式，以及对数据授权和专业工作流的深远影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unsung.aresluna.org/they-had-no-concept-of-a-duty-of-care-to-their-users/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On caring for user data: NeoVim caused Vim undo files to be deleted</span><span class='zh'>关于对用户数据的关怀：NeoVim 导致 Vim 的撤销文件被删除</span></a><span class="dom">unsung.aresluna.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A data-care incident shows NeoVim inadvertently deleting Vim undo files, prompting a reevaluation of cross-project data safety and the duty of care in software design.</span><span class='zh'>一次数据关怀事件暴露 NeoVim 无意中删除了 Vim 的撤销文件，促使人们重新评估跨项目的数据安全和软件设计中的关怀义务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A data-care incident shows NeoVim inadvertently deleting Vim undo files, prompting a reevaluation of cross-project data safety and the duty of care in software design. The piece argues for clearer expectations around user data preservation and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>一次数据关怀事件暴露 NeoVim 无意中删除了 Vim 的撤销文件，促使人们重新评估跨项目的数据安全和软件设计中的关怀义务。文章呼吁在用户数据保留与跨版本兼容方面设定更清晰的期望。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The MongoDB CEO is stepping down to join Meta, signaling a leadership shift between database infrastructure and a major tech platform.</span><span class='zh'>MongoDB 的首席执行官宣布辞职，加入 Meta，标志着数据库基础设施领域高层人才向大型科技平台的流动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The MongoDB CEO is stepping down to join Meta, signaling a leadership shift between database infrastructure and a major tech platform. The move could influence MongoDB’s strategy and underscores Meta’s push to strengthen its enterprise tooling and data stack.</span><span class='zh'>MongoDB 的首席执行官宣布辞职，加入 Meta，标志着数据库基础设施领域高层人才向大型科技平台的流动。此举可能影响 MongoDB 的战略方向，也反映 Meta 在企业级工具与数据栈方面的布局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dwarfstar.sh/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>From the creator of Redis; run LLM locally with ds4</span><span class='zh'>来自 Redis 创始人之手；用 ds4 本地运行 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">dwarfstar.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>From the creator of Redis, ds4 is a local LLM runner designed to execute inference on commodity hardware, avoiding cloud latency and preserving privacy.</span><span class='zh'>来自 Redis 创始人之手的 ds4 是一个本地大型语言模型推理工具，旨在在普通硬件上离线运行、避免云端延迟并提升隐私性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>From the creator of Redis, ds4 is a local LLM runner designed to execute inference on commodity hardware, avoiding cloud latency and preserving privacy. It targets efficient memory usage and practical offline experimentation with open models, lowering the barrier to private AI pilots.</span><span class='zh'>来自 Redis 创始人之手的 ds4 是一个本地大型语言模型推理工具，旨在在普通硬件上离线运行、避免云端延迟并提升隐私性。它强调高效的内存使用，降低了私有 AI 试验的门槛。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google appears to be backing away from its pledge of 10 years of software updates for Chromebooks, potentially shortening the support window for many devices. The move could disrupt long-life deployment plans in schools and businesses and raise security and depreciation concerns as devices age.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌似乎正在削减对 Chromebook 10 年更新承诺的落实，可能缩短许多设备的支持周期。这一举动或将扰乱学校和企业的长期部署计划，并在设备老化时带来安全与折旧方面的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://erictopol.substack.com/p/loss-of-cell-identity-drives-human" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Loss of cell identity drives human aging: Two new papers</span><span class='zh'>细胞身份丧失推动人类衰老：两篇新论文</span></a><span class="dom">erictopol.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two papers argue that loss of cell identity drives aging, with cells gradually losing their specialized programs.</span><span class='zh'>两篇论文认为，细胞身份的丧失推动衰老，细胞逐渐偏离特定组织的专门化程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two papers argue that loss of cell identity drives aging, with cells gradually losing their specialized programs. Reasserting or reprogramming cell identity could open new anti-aging strategies that go beyond traditional longevity approaches.</span><span class='zh'>两篇论文认为，细胞身份的丧失推动衰老，细胞逐渐偏离特定组织的专门化程序。重建或重新编程细胞身份可能开启新的抗衧老策略，超越传统的延缓寿命方法。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A counterpoint to memory-augmented agents, arguing that structured, queryable documentation can be more reliable than raw memory for AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>提出代理人不需要大规模记忆，而需要结构化、可查询的文档。</span></p>

The piece advocates embedding knowledge in accessible artifacts to improve reasoning and auditability in AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>提出代理人不需要大规模记忆，而需要结构化、可查询的文档。文章建议将知识嵌入可访问的资料中，以提升可靠性、可审计性和可解释性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ntfargo/Relapse-Exploit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PS5 Relapse Exploit</span><span class='zh'>PS5 Relapse 漏洞利用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relapse appears to be a PS5 vulnerability exploit that enables arbitrary code execution on affected firmware, potentially allowing homebrew or custom firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Relapse 漏洞利用工具可能在受影响的固件上实现任意代码执行，或允许自制固件和自制程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relapse appears to be a PS5 vulnerability exploit that enables arbitrary code execution on affected firmware, potentially allowing homebrew or custom firmware. The discovery underscores persistent security risks in console ecosystems and the ongoing race between protection and exploitation.</span><span class='zh'>Relapse 漏洞利用工具可能在受影响的固件上实现任意代码执行，或允许自制固件和自制程序。此发现再次凸显游戏主机生态系统的长期安全风险，以及防护与利用之间的博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/0xcrypto/status/2105515822643114182" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fuck Android Developer Verification Program</span><span class='zh'>强烈反对 Android 开发者验证计划</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative post voices strong opposition to Google&#x27;s Android Developer Verification Program, arguing the policy creates friction for developers and raises concerns about privacy and gatekeeping.</span><span class='zh'>这条观点明确表达对 Android 开发者验证计划的强烈不满，认为该计划增加了开发者的门槛并引发隐私与平台把关等担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative post voices strong opposition to Google&#x27;s Android Developer Verification Program, arguing the policy creates friction for developers and raises concerns about privacy and gatekeeping. The discussion highlights ongoing tensions between platform control and developer-friendly onboarding.</span><span class='zh'>这条观点明确表达对 Android 开发者验证计划的强烈不满，认为该计划增加了开发者的门槛并引发隐私与平台把关等担忧。讨论反映出平台控制与开发者体验之间的持续张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://git.mills.io/prologic/parley" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Parley: Federated, decentralised chat that speaks plain IRC</span><span class='zh'>Parley：联邦化、去中心化的聊天，像 IRC 一样简单</span></a><span class="dom">git.mills.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Parley federates chat across servers using an IRC-like protocol, offering a decentralized alternative to centralized chat apps.</span><span class='zh'>Parley 将聊天跨服务器联邦化，使用类 IRC 的协议，提供一个去中心化的替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Parley federates chat across servers using an IRC-like protocol, offering a decentralized alternative to centralized chat apps. It emphasizes user ownership and interoperability over platform-lock-in. Adoption will depend on ecosystem tooling and client support.</span><span class='zh'>Parley 将聊天跨服务器联邦化，使用类 IRC 的协议，提供一个去中心化的替代方案。它强调用户掌控和互操作性，而非被单一平台锁定。能否被广泛采用取决于生态工具和客户端支持的成熟度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NnV_cWeoo5Q" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kroah-Hartman – Security in the LLM Age [video]</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kroah-Hartman：LLM时代的安全性 [视频]</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Greg Kroah-Hartman discusses security in the LLM age, highlighting new risks from large models, prompts, and supply chains.</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kroah-Hartman 讨论了在 LLM 时代的安全挑战，聚焦大模型、提示攻击和供应链等新风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Greg Kroah-Hartman discusses security in the LLM age, highlighting new risks from large models, prompts, and supply chains. He argues for stronger containment, safer interfaces, and better tooling for kernel and system security as AI becomes integral to software. The talk offers practical takeaways for developers building AI-assisted systems.</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kroah-Hartman 讨论了在 LLM 时代的安全挑战，聚焦大模型、提示攻击和供应链等新风险。他主张加强容器化与隔离、改进接口安全，以及在内核与系统层提供更好的安全工具。此视频为构建 AI 助力系统的开发者提供了切实可行的建议。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple updates macOS full disk access permissions, adding more granular prompts and directory-scoped controls.</span><span class='zh'>苹果对 macOS 的全磁盘访问权限进行了更新，增加了更细粒度的提示和基于目录的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple updates macOS full disk access permissions, adding more granular prompts and directory-scoped controls. The changes push developers toward transparent data usage and safer defaults, reshaping how apps request broad file-system access.</span><span class='zh'>苹果对 macOS 的全磁盘访问权限进行了更新，增加了更细粒度的提示和基于目录的控制。改动促使开发者在数据使用上更透明、默认设置更安全，从而重塑应用请求广域文件系统访问的方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS 27&#x27;s AI features can consume significant disk space through caches and data.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 27 的 Apple Intelligence 功能会占用磁盘空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS 27&#x27;s AI features can consume significant disk space through caches and data. A community-made guide shows how to disable Apple Intelligence to reclaim storage, with a trade-off in AI-assisted functionality.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 27 的 Apple Intelligence 功能会占用磁盘空间。通过关闭这一功能，可以释放存储，但可能会牺牲部分 AI 辅助功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.space.com/space-exploration/launches-spacecraft/spacexs-starship-megarocket-launching-to-orbit-for-1st-time-ever-on-sept-28-watch-it-live" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX&#x27;s Starship launching to orbit for first time ever today</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射</span></a><span class="dom">space.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starship&#x27;s first orbital flight marks a landmark for fully reusable space launch tech, testing end-to-end performance from fueling to recovery.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射标志着可完全重复使用的太空发射技术的里程碑，涵盖从加注到回收的端到端测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starship&#x27;s first orbital flight marks a landmark for fully reusable space launch tech, testing end-to-end performance from fueling to recovery. Success or failure will influence future satellite deployment, lunar and Mars ambitions, and investor sentiment.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射标志着可完全重复使用的太空发射技术的里程碑，涵盖从加注到回收的端到端测试。成败都将影响未来卫星部署、月球与火星任务的前景，以及投资者情绪。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.faav.net/how-i-couldve-accessed-17-trillion-microsoft-records" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I could&#x27;ve accessed 17T Microsoft records</span><span class='zh'>我本来可以访问17万亿条微软记录</span></a><span class="dom">blog.faav.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A disclosure-style post argues that misconfigured access points could expose an enormous trove of Microsoft records, underscoring the fragility of giant data stores.</span><span class='zh'>作者声称通过某些错误配置可以访问17万亿条微软记录，凸显巨型数据仓库的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A disclosure-style post argues that misconfigured access points could expose an enormous trove of Microsoft records, underscoring the fragility of giant data stores. It calls for stronger access controls, auditing, and responsible disclosure practices.</span><span class='zh'>作者声称通过某些错误配置可以访问17万亿条微软记录，凸显巨型数据仓库的脆弱性。文章强调需要更强的访问控制、更完善的审计与负责任的披露流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tcl-lang.org/software/tcltk/9.1.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tcl/Tk 9.1</span><span class='zh'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 发布</span></a><span class="dom">tcl-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 brings incremental improvements to both the scripting language and the Tk GUI toolkit, focusing on stability and cross-platform consistency.</span><span class='zh'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 在脚本语言与 Tk GUI 工具包上带来渐进改进，重点提升稳定性和跨平台一致性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 brings incremental improvements to both the scripting language and the Tk GUI toolkit, focusing on stability and cross-platform consistency. Developers can expect better packaging, refreshed widgets, and smoother interoperability with modern runtimes. The update reinforces Tcl/Tk’s role for lightweight, scriptable desktop UIs.</span><span class='zh'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 在脚本语言与 Tk GUI 工具包上带来渐进改进，重点提升稳定性和跨平台一致性。开发者将享受更好的打包、更新的控件以及与现代运行时的更好互操作性。更新强化了 Tcl/Tk 在轻量化、可脚本化桌面 UI 领域的地位。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gwern.net/doc/math/1973-halmos.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Legend of von Neumann (1973) [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>冯·诺依曼传奇（1973）[pdf]</span></a><span class="dom">gwern.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concise retrospective essay revisits the legend of John von Neumann, separating folklore from reality.</span><span class='zh'>这篇简短的回顾性随笔区分了冯·诺依曼传说与真实贡献，梳理他在数学与计算领域的广泛贡献，并反思他的形象如何塑造了人们对计算的认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concise retrospective essay revisits the legend of John von Neumann, separating folklore from reality. It surveys his wide-ranging contributions to mathematics and computing and reflects on how his persona helped shape the way we think about computation.</span><span class='zh'>这篇简短的回顾性随笔区分了冯·诺依曼传说与真实贡献，梳理他在数学与计算领域的广泛贡献，并反思他的形象如何塑造了人们对计算的认知。</span></p>

It&#x27;s a compact, hands-on lens for engineers and enthusiasts interested in product history.</span><span class='zh'>Shimano自行车博物馆通过展品展示了从早期齿轮到现代传动系统的演变。评测强调了Shimano在机械设计和骑行体验上的影响，揭示了遗产与创新在自行车文化中的交汇。对于工程师和爱好者而言，这是窥见产品史的紧凑视角。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Before pixels argues for modular, data-driven dashboards in industrial settings, arguing that composable UI components beat pixel-perfect, one-off dashboards.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在工业场景中采用模块化、数据驱动的仪表板，以替代追求像素级完美的单一界面。通过讨论如何将传感器数据、设备遥测和分析组件解耦组合，提升操作员的可视性和系统鲁棒性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sujithjay.com/inventing-work" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Staff Engineer&#x27;s Guide to Inventing Work</span><span class='zh'>一名资深工程师的工作创新指南</span></a><span class="dom">sujithjay.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A staff engineer shares a framework for identifying valuable problems, shaping teams, and building durable, impactful work.</span><span class='zh'>这位资深工程师分享了在大型团队中如何发现具价值的问题、组织协作并产出可持续、具影响力的工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A staff engineer shares a framework for identifying valuable problems, shaping teams, and building durable, impactful work. The guide emphasizes ownership, mentorship, and scalable patterns for invention within a product org.</span><span class='zh'>这位资深工程师分享了在大型团队中如何发现具价值的问题、组织协作并产出可持续、具影响力的工作。指南强调所有权、导师制和可扩展的工作模式在产品化组织中的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iain.rocks/blog/dont-couple-your-go-code-to-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t couple your Go code to GitHub</span><span class='zh'>不要把 Go 代码与 GitHub 捆绑在一起</span></a><span class="dom">iain.rocks</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues against tightly coupling Go projects to GitHub, advocating for alternative hosting, CI strategies, and dependency management to improve resilience and portability.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张不要让 Go 项目过度绑定到 GitHub，建议采用替代托管、CI 策略与依赖管理来提升韧性与可移植性，并考虑灾难恢复与供应商独立性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues against tightly coupling Go projects to GitHub, advocating for alternative hosting, CI strategies, and dependency management to improve resilience and portability. It urges thinking about disaster recovery and vendor independence.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张不要让 Go 项目过度绑定到 GitHub，建议采用替代托管、CI 策略与依赖管理来提升韧性与可移植性，并考虑灾难恢复与供应商独立性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS: A Vulkan Reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 Neural Rendering Network</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR：基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs. If successful, it could empower developers to integrate neural upscaling into Vulkan apps without relying on Nvidia&#x27;s stack, but performance and fidelity benchmarks remain uncertain.</span><span class='zh'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。若成熟可让开发者在 Vulkan 应用中实现神经上采样，而不必依赖 Nvidia 的专有栈，但性能和画质的基准仍在发展中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/magnitudedev/magnitude" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – Self-optimizing inference engine for agents</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – 面向代理的自我优化推理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency. It sits at the intersection of AI agent frameworks and efficient inference, promising to reduce manual tuning and hardware waste. Backed by YC S25, it signals growing interest in tooling for autonomous agents.</span><span class='zh'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。它处在 AI 代理框架与高效推理技术的交汇处，代表了为自走代理提升算力利用率的一条新路径。由 YC S25 支持，反映了对自动化代理工具的热切关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andreasfertig/cppinsights" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C++ Insights – See your source code with the eyes of a Compiler</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights：用编译器的视角看你的源代码</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action. This helps developers understand surprising behavior and write more robust, well-abstracted code.</span><span class='zh'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。它帮助开发者理解意料之外的行为，并编写更健壮、抽象层次更清晰的代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dashersw/coyopedal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Guitar amp and effects pedal built on the Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-2.06</span><span class='zh'>基于 Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-2.06 的吉他放大器与效果器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on project builds a guitar amp and effects pedal around the ESP32-S3 with a touch display, showcasing how affordable microcontrollers can power portable, customizable audio gear.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用 ESP32-S3 和触控显示屏打造吉他放大器与效果器的动手项目，展示了低成本微控制器如何实现便携、可定制的音频设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on project builds a guitar amp and effects pedal around the ESP32-S3 with a touch display, showcasing how affordable microcontrollers can power portable, customizable audio gear. It demonstrates hardware hacking, real-time audio processing, and DIY creativity.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用 ESP32-S3 和触控显示屏打造吉他放大器与效果器的动手项目，展示了低成本微控制器如何实现便携、可定制的音频设备。项目融合硬件黑客、实时音频处理与创意实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kvoltmer/Audionaut" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Audionaut – an open-source cross-platform multitrack audio editor</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Audionaut——一个开源跨平台的多轨音频编辑器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Audionaut is an open-source, cross-platform multitrack audio editor that aims to empower creators with a community-driven alternative to proprietary tools.</span><span class='zh'>Audionaut 是一个开源、跨平台的多轨音频编辑器，旨在为创作者提供开源替代品，促进社区协作与快速迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Audionaut is an open-source, cross-platform multitrack audio editor that aims to empower creators with a community-driven alternative to proprietary tools. It supports recording, editing, and mixing workflows across Windows, macOS, and Linux, inviting collaboration and rapid feature iteration. This project signals a broader move toward open-source, customizable audio tooling for indie producers.</span><span class='zh'>Audionaut 是一个开源、跨平台的多轨音频编辑器，旨在为创作者提供开源替代品，促进社区协作与快速迭代。它支持 Windows、macOS 和 Linux，覆盖录制、编辑、混音等工作流程，鼓励共同开发新功能。该项目代表了开源音频工具在独立创作者生态中的潜在增长。</span></p>

This approach can halve build and check times, sharpening feedback for changes and guiding compiler design.</span><span class='zh'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。此举对构建系统和编译器设计也有启示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepfates/imp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems. The project enables BEAM friendly probabilistic programming workflows and leverages the actor model for parallelism across cores.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。该项目为 BEAM 上的概率编程和并行工作流提供更友好的实现，利用 actor 模型在多核上实现并行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vibra-Ingenn/Janus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Janus is a Go-based binary that runs GGUF-formatted models via Vulkan across major GPUs, enabling local AI inference with a single cross-platform tool.</span><span class='zh'>Janus 是一个用 Go 编写的二进制程序，借助 Vulkan 在 AMD/Intel/Nvidia 等显卡上运行 GGUF 模型，实现本地推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Janus is a Go-based binary that runs GGUF-formatted models via Vulkan across major GPUs, enabling local AI inference with a single cross-platform tool. It streamlines setup, supports multiple backends, and lowers the barrier for experiments on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Janus 是一个用 Go 编写的二进制程序，借助 Vulkan 在 AMD/Intel/Nvidia 等显卡上运行 GGUF 模型，实现本地推理。它简化了安装流程，支持多种后端，并降低了在消费级硬件上进行实验的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/edgcpp/compiler" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EDG C++ Compiler is open source</span><span class='zh'>EDG C++ 编译器开源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EDG&#x27;s C++ compiler frontend has been open-sourced on GitHub, giving researchers and tool builders access to a mature parsing pipeline that underpins many commercial toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>EDG 的 C++ 编译器前端已在 GitHub 开源，开发者和研究者现在可以访问成熟的解析管线，其广泛支撑着多家商用工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EDG&#x27;s C++ compiler frontend has been open-sourced on GitHub, giving researchers and tool builders access to a mature parsing pipeline that underpins many commercial toolchains. The move lowers barriers for experimentation with C++ frontends, static analysis, and language tooling, and could influence compiler ecosystem dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>EDG 的 C++ 编译器前端已在 GitHub 开源，开发者和研究者现在可以访问成熟的解析管线，其广泛支撑着多家商用工具链。此举降低了对 C++ 前端、静态分析和语言工具开发的门槛，可能影响编译器生态的演进与竞争格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ahmd-sh/hntui" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hntui – A TUI for Hacker News</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hntui——用于 Hacker News 的文本用户界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hntui is a Show HN project that implements a terminal user interface for Hacker News, enabling fast keyboard-driven reading in a lightweight app.</span><span class='zh'>Hntui 是一个 Show HN 项目，为 Hacker News 构建了一个终端文本用户界面，实现快捷的键盘驱动阅读。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hntui is a Show HN project that implements a terminal user interface for Hacker News, enabling fast keyboard-driven reading in a lightweight app. It demonstrates how a well-crafted TUI can deliver a distraction-free browsing experience. A proof-of-concept worth watching for CLI UI enthusiasts.</span><span class='zh'>Hntui 是一个 Show HN 项目，为 Hacker News 构建了一个终端文本用户界面，实现快捷的键盘驱动阅读。它展示了精心设计的文本界面如何提供更专注的浏览体验。对于命令行界面爱好者来说是一个值得关注的原型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TantrixAuto/yantra" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yantra – an LALR(1) parser generator for C++</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yantra — 面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yantra is a new LALR(1) parser generator for C++.</span><span class='zh'>Yantra 是一个面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器，旨在通过输出整洁、符合现代 C++ 风格的代码与解析表来简化解析器开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yantra is a new LALR(1) parser generator for C++. It aims to streamline building language parsers by emitting clean, idiomatic C++ code and parsing tables. For projects where tight integration with modern C++ tooling matters, it offers an alternative to traditional parser generators with a focus on ergonomics and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Yantra 是一个面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器，旨在通过输出整洁、符合现代 C++ 风格的代码与解析表来简化解析器开发。在需要与现代 C++ 工具链紧密集成的场景中，它提供了一个对传统生成器的高效、易用的替代方案。</span></p>
